Article 5 is provided to protect the privacy of private life and to ensure the effective protection of personality rights. In the 244th Session of the Federal German Assembly, the Netzwerkdurchssetz (NetzDG) Law on improving law enforcement on social networks with a joint proposal of CDU, CSU and SPD law was adopted for this purpose, which was adopted on 1 October 2017, but it was required by network providers to report the perpetrators with IP addresses to the police on June 19, 2020. This law is similar to our Law number 5651 in the Turkish legal system, but according to Law 5651 in the German Social Network Law, it has provided much more obligation to social network providers, for those businesses, high administrative fines if they fail to meet those obligations. The amount of fines imposed by the Federal German Ministry of Justice is up to 50m euros. tbmm-2020-07-28sit02spe0236par0658	Thank you, Mr. President. I salute the General Assembly. I'd like to thank our lawmakers for asking questions. The question of Mr. Özdemir is: Will the limitation of social media in an environment where there is no security of law not be found as a flaw in the context of contracts internationally? First of all, when we examine the proposal, it is also possible to see that the proposal contains regulations related to legal safety, protection of personal data, protection of private life, freedom of communication, freedom of speech, freedom of expression and distribution of thought, and that these include protective arrangements. The 20th article of our Constitution on the privacy of private life, the 8th article of the European Convention on Human Rights, the 17th article of the United Nations Civil and Political Rights Convention, the 22nd article of our Constitution, which regulates the freedom of communication again, the 10th article of the European Convention on Human Rights, particularly the 10th article of the Constitution, which protects and protects the rights of the person in the field of social media, especially in the area of which protects the rights of the European Convention on Human Rights. In particular, our Law 5651 required revisions in order to regulate Internet publications and prevent Internet crime. With this offer, we see these revisions being made. Of course, limiting our rights and freedoms is subject to certain conditions in our Constitution. National security, public order, prevention of crime, protection of private life, protection of individual rights, protection of other people's rights and freedoms, of course, can be constrained by law. These constraints in the proposal are also constraints in accordance with our Constitution, and limits in accordance with international contracts. During the commission's work, the amendment motions were issued, and we had concluded the debate on the Constitutional objection claims, and the Commission decided that it was not contrary to the Constitution, according to Article 38 of the Civil Code, and had just been expressed during general negotiations. Of course, our lawmakers who prepared the proposal took advantage of the academics, the enforcers, and they explained that they had taken advantage of it, and they're talking about it as a reason for the proposal of the law. Regarding the verdicts made by the magistrates, the question of Mr. Hawkaan is: Yes, this offer is given to the judicial justices and to the previous Law 5651. Of course, when you make these decisions, shouldn't there be wrong decisions in the justices of the peace? Maybe, but of course there are ways to fix it. They have objections to this verdict, they have the right to appeal to an upper court, they have the right to bring it to a higher court, they have the right to take it to the Constitutional Court, even to the European Court of Human Rights. The question of the storage of data and how it will be controlled: Of course, this is a new area of social media regulations; the issue of the storage of data, the issue of the protection of personal data. Of course, we're all on social media right now, hiding data from these social media companies. An international arrangement, an international contract, the state's own internal law regulations are all new issues. The issues we discussed today at the General Assembly, yes, Germany has done them, organised them, Turkey is now organizing them, tomorrow other countries... Internal law regulations will follow. And then, of course, it's clear, internationally, that social media is not an irresponsible area in terms of international contracts, that protective rights and freedoms should be carried out in the context of international contracts. And in fact, everyone agrees. Mr. Ozer asked about the purpose of the social media arrangement; for what purpose do you perform it? Of course, that's what I'm talking about. So, when we look at the proposal in summary, the previous law was not defined by the social network provider, it's defined here. Again, it is necessary to have representatives in Turkey; social media companies with more than a million accesses are obliged to have representatives in Turkey. And if not, then it's gradually organised; it starts with a fine, it goes up to the ban of advertising, and then there's a matter that goes down to narrowing the tape by the magistrate's decision, but it doesn't completely ban social media, but it doesn't completely block access. Again, arrangements are being made to determine in terms of the rights to personality and the violation of privacy, in which cases people will apply for answers. Again, some obligations are being brought to social network providers, content providers, about reporting. An arrangement is being brought to house the data of its users in Turkey; a very important arrangement. And in terms of notification -- of course, these companies are abroad -- there's going to be some problems. There are arrangements to address these problems. Again, a more secure system for expression and freedom of information is being brought here in the arrangement for the extraction of content. An important provision is being brought in not to completely block access to the site, but to remove that content and prevent access from being blocked. Again, there is also an opportunity for the decision to remove or block access to the content given in the case of violation of personal rights, as well as for the decision not to associate the person's rights with the infringement content and the name of the applicants, the right to be forgotten. The administrative fines for those who don't report residential and fail to meet the obligations of the law are also organised in this law. So, the purpose of this social media arrangement is to solve the problems that have arisen in our Law on the Editing of social media in Internet Media No. 5651 and the Fighting Crimes Through These Broadcasts.
